- **D-446** — The language model resolves how the user names a passage; no index is built and the kinds of reference are not enumerated *(`a ratification event named`)*
- **D-447** — The model's tool definitions are generated from the operation set, never maintained by hand *(`a ratification event named`)*
- **D-448** — The operation set is curated from observed use, not an exposure of every editing method *(`a ratification event named`)*
- **D-449** — Factor granularity is fixed: the bass factor is evaluated per event, the missing-tone penalty per event of segment length, the emission per tone, and the boundary-family factors per boundary *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-450** — The key-signature and declared-mode prior conditions the INITIAL key state only, re-entering only at a notated signature change *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-451** — A desk simulation's table values are provisional, enter no fit, and a verdict that would flip inside a provisional value's plausible range is reported as a near-tie, never as a win *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-452** — Every desk-simulation trace runs at identity weights — the ratified ablation baseline — so the trace tests the structure and the tables, not the weighting *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-453** — The desk simulation's verdict: the ratified factorization passes nine of ten traces and no finding reopens the structure *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-454** — The grouping layer detects nothing — it assembles what earlier layers decided, and pressure to add detection means the work belongs elsewhere *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-455** — A cadence away from a grouping boundary is surfaced as internal, never snapped to the nearest boundary and never discarded *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-456** — Sections, periods and sentences are out of the grouping layer's core for PROPORTIONALITY — not disqualified for lacking an oracle *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-457** — A group truncated by the selection edge is marked as truncated, and a group that runs off the edge unclosed carries an extension cue the grouping layer only surfaces *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-458** — The codetta refinement is read as the as-built tiling: keep the strong cut, drop the weak one, and record the codetta end as an annexe *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-459** — The key-area confidence is a declared margin-class boundary confidence, and its input is the declared key confidence — never the grading diagnostics' sigmoid *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-460** — A group counts as fully resolved exactly when no unit in it carries an unresolved mark — no confidence threshold enters the test *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-461** — The grouping layer is an explainability layer, not an accuracy requirement, and is deliberately kept thin *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-462** — Cadence validation is scoped to LOCATION; cadence TYPE is only partially attributable and is never a clean gate *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-463** — The temporal signals sitting in the vertical scorer are left where they are, and the gate that depends on them must move with them *(`a ratification event named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-464** — No further progression-level signal may be added to the single-step look-around structure; it goes in the progression context instead *(`a ratification event named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-465** — The policy for judging a proposed post-scoring gate: another bias correction gets the bias fixed first, a structural condition is sound, and a cascade means the missing thing is functional context *(`a ratification event named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-466** — Forward-only is a strong DEFAULT, not dogma — a backward edge is admissible only as a deliberate, surfaced, measured, documented exception *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-467** — A rebuilt or re-tuned chord scoring must not rely on the held-note repetition bonus the faithful note model removed *(`a ratification event named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-468** — The pinned block-(A) instrument declares which inference arm its baselines were measured on, and refuses a corpus whose stamp disagrees *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-469** — The tick-local path is left OUTSIDE the unified pipeline by design — its point-in-time semantics would be distorted by one shared interface *(`a ratification event named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-470** — The temporal-context extension fields are recorded during the pipeline's own analysis pass; no consumer re-runs the chord analysis to rebuild them *(`a ratification event named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-471** — The sub-beat annotation duration gate is not retired on argument — it is kept or dropped on a measured observation run, with the verdict stated in advance *(`a ratification event named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-472** — Key areas are grouped by a smoothing pass over regions whose key sequence has already been smoothed, and a region that disagrees without clearing the confidence test keeps its own key while being grouped into the enclosing area *(`a numbered ruling named+a ratification event named+a ruling record named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-473** — A theory-grounding pass labels every load-bearing claim FACT / THEORY / CONJECTURE, cross-checks its central sources independently, and carries no equation out of a text it could not fetch *(`a ratification event named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-474** — No published study reports per-axis inter-annotator agreement for Roman-numeral analysis of Baroque/classical symbolic music — the ground-truth ceiling principle #21 demands is unmeasured by the entire field *(`a ratification event named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-475** — The BCMH chorale annotations are NOT established as an instrument: one named annotator with no independent second annotation, the annotations sit on a reduction, and they reached the repository through a machine translation *(`a numbered ruling named+a ratification event named+a ruling record named`)*
- **D-476** — The phrase-boundary primitive is owned by the notation-derived view layer — not by the note model, and not by the function layer that consumes it *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-477** — Phrase boundaries are read from the written surface alone — never from a resolved key, chord or cadence — and the boundaries this misses are accepted, not recovered here *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-478** — A phrase boundary is a peak in a continuous boundary-strength profile, not the OR of a few binary signals *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-479** — The boundary cues run per eligible voice and aggregate to the texture, and BOTH the per-voice and the texture boundaries are published *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-480** — The phrase-boundary primitive is NOT an accuracy requirement — a competitive reference engine does no phrase segmentation at all — so it is built right but kept proportionate *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-481** — The notated markers are emitted as boundaries unconditionally; only the surface-cue strength is peak-picked *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-482** — The two hand-synchronised copies of the fermata scan retire into one owned primitive, and that retirement changes no output *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-483** — The picked boundaries are validated against the analysts' own phrase marks; a fermata-derived phrase list is inadmissible as ground truth because the primitive reads fermatas *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-484** — The phrase-boundary primitive is a derived view: it inherits the loaded span, requests no extension of its own, and publishes a per-profile max-normalised boundary confidence *(`a numbered ruling named+a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-485** — Each picked boundary should carry which cue fired and at what scope; the picked set is scope-blind today and the refinement waits for the inference phase *(`a numbered ruling named+a ratification event named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-486** — A measurement publishes its coverage denominator and its per-corpus breakdown; a single aggregate number that hides which corpus moved is not reported *(`a ratification event named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-487** — The eleven snapshot source scores are frozen and hash-pinned; changing the set or bumping a pin is a deliberate golden and gate re-baseline *(`a ratification event named`)*
- **D-488** — The two Bach chorale collections are independent selections, not sub- and superset — and the diff between them is not recoverable in-repo *(`a ratification event named`)*
- **D-489** — The snapshot sources are hash-pinned rather than copied in-tree, because their licences make an in-tree copy incompatible with this project's licence *(`a ratification event named`)*
- **D-490** — FALSIFIED: no threshold can make the fine-grain function override net-positive — the harm rate is flat against both quantities the threshold is built from *(`a ratification event named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-491** — REFUTED: making the override's comparison vertically fair does not repair it — even where the alternative fits the notes at least as well, it is still about 71 % harmful *(`a ratification event named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-492** — The recommended redesign is to demote the override to an annotation — carrying the earlier reading unchanged and surfacing the contradiction — floored by simply disabling it *(`a ratification event named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-493** — Restricting the override to the genuinely-coupled key-and-chord minority is UN-COMPUTABLE, not merely unmeasured: its trigger is not computed anywhere and building it is the still-owed joint step *(`a ratification event named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-494** — RATIFIED AMENDMENT A-4: the function layer must gain key-confirmation channels that do not require a cadence, plus an enharmonic-identity rule for key spans *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-495** — RATIFIED AMENDMENT A-5: when the phrase-boundary profile is flat, cadence admission relaxes with vote-weight scaling instead of starving *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-496** — RATIFIED AMENDMENT A-6: whether the pairwise progression grammar lives inside the harmonic vocabulary or stays a separate store is decided at the recognition-consumer build, explicitly *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-497** — RATIFIED AMENDMENT A-7: the empirically-unvalidated mark must be APPLIED to the Jazz preset constants and the unvalidated idioms, with the validation path named *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-498** — RATIFIED AMENDMENT A-9: a product stance is owed for output that is mostly uncertain, and for music outside the tonal vocabulary altogether *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-499** — RATIFIED AMENDMENT A-10: four documentation riders — a consolidated ownership page for the notation-derived views, the membership tie-breaker recorded as idiom-calibrated, and the producer-agnostic seam pinned as a design property *(`a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-500** — The user ratified CORPUS EXPANSION at the architecture review: gate-grade jazz ground truth, chromatic material of the Wagner class, and more non-Bach, non-Baroque annotation generally *(`a numbered ruling named+a ratification event named+a recorded ratifier+a ruling record named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-501** — A tool may read a written chord symbol ONLY as a comparison or ground-truth label — never as input that influences what the analyzer computes *(`a ratification event named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-502** — The span a recognised named progression covers is called the progression-schema-span — the bare word 'sequence' is reserved for the harmonic sequence and 'progression' for the whole committed chord stream *(`a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-503** — The idiom mixture is DISCOVERED from the score and merely SEEDED by the user's preset, in three forward-only phases *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-504** — A recognised harmonic sequence is ALWAYS emitted as key evidence — the earlier gate that emitted it only where no cadence existed threw corroboration away *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-505** — A harmonic sequence requires at least two transposed statements of the SAME recognised entry; a single internally-sequential entry emits none *(`a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-506** — Progression recognition is ADDITIVE: the literal Roman numeral is never rewritten, and a substitution is recorded only in the annotation *(`a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-507** — A catalog entry defined by its melodic or bass lines is recognised by its chord skeleton alone and carries a 'chords-only' mark, with its prior strength reduced *(`a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-508** — The catalog/grammar consistency test ships scoped to the MEASURED containment — an explicit known-gap list — and tightens to a clean assertion when the grammar amendment lands *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-509** — Where the analysis already committed a chord, a recognised progression corrects it through the EXISTING override frame and may only SELECT an already-carried reading — no new comparison frame, and never a reading built from the notes *(`a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-510** — The correct carry is the one that keeps the distinct alternative reading, not the one that appends a near-duplicate of the winner — chosen on the carry's purpose, not on which code is at HEAD *(`a user-act marker`)*
- **D-511** — One promotion primitive with a present-first dedup guard replaces the two ad-hoc promotion idioms; the append branch fires only when the target is genuinely absent *(`a user-act marker`)*
- **D-512** — Gate A becomes removable only once the unified promotion reproduces its carry byte-for-byte — that reproduction IS the retirement condition, not the winner-inertness that preceded it *(`a user-act marker`)*
- **D-513** — A corpus registry's content summary is enumeration provenance, not evidence that an annotation layer is present — per-slice presence must be measured *(`a numbered ruling named+a ruling record named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-514** — A newly acquired annotation set whose works OVERLAP the regression corpus is RECORD-ONLY: it may not be wired to, compared against, or bulk-diffed with the gate corpus without a user ruling *(`a numbered ruling named+a ruling record named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-515** — A ground-truth class with a named consumer gets its OWN row in the needs vector, never a remark under a neighbouring row — ruled generally after being decided for pedal-point ground truth *(`a numbered ruling named+a recorded ratifier+a ruling record named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-516** — Two ground-truth classes with named consumers but no needs row were ADOPTED at the first full-needs audit — contrapuntal/imitative structure, and marked part-writing errors *(`a numbered ruling named+a recorded ratifier+a ruling record named`)*
- **D-521** — The general law of the circularity map: an abstract circle becomes acyclic in the concrete by one of four named conditions — and every alleged circle in this system fell to one of them *(`a user-act marker`)*
- **D-522** — Explaining an inference to the end user is a late-bound DISPLAY consumer of facts that already exist — not a new analysis *(`a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-524** — The joint state's mode axis is TWO modes — major and composite minor; modal and chromatic colour lives in the pitch emission, and the un-rounded reading is published *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-525** — The fit is STAGED: the factor tables are counted from ground truth and frozen, and only a small vector of combination weights is fit discriminatively — with an all-weights-equal ablation arm that must be beaten *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-526** — The joint state's chord axis is SCALE-DEGREE-VALUED — a Roman numeral relative to the state's own tonic and mode — and the chord symbol is a DERIVED fact published once *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-527** — There is NO live non-chord-tone cleaning stage: each tone is emitted by category inside the one decode, conditioned on chord-independent melodic and metric covariates, and ornament labels are derived AFTER it *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-528** — The key signature and declared mode enter as a WEAK FITTED SOFT PRIOR with no conditional gate anywhere — the probability calculus delivers 'consult it only when unsure', and the hard declared-mode wall is formally retired *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-531** — The hand-built emission is CONFIRMED and the learned replacement is NOT triggered — retained as an explicit fallback with a concrete trigger, and scoped to one repertoire with a named re-check gate *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-532** — The chord-transition table gains one pooling level that groups a secondary dominant's continuations by their RELATION to its target — restoring from counts the one behaviour that defines the chord class *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-533** — A continuation too rare to have its own stored probability is scored by dividing the row's leftover in PROPORTION to each chord's overall frequency — never evenly, and never as impossible *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-534** — The penalty for a chord tone that never sounds is COUNTED per chord factor — root, third, fifth, seventh — replacing one invented blanket number; the per-factor asymmetry then comes free *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-535** — The checking stage's verdict: the real counted tables overturn no desk-simulation verdict, but margins moved in both directions and one margin expectation was plainly wrong *(`a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-536** — The bass note and the chord are chosen TOGETHER — the winner is the (bass, root, template) triple — replacing the sequential commit-the-bass-then-score pipeline *(`a user-act marker`)*
- **D-537** — The completeness bonus fires ONLY for a root-position reading whose three triad tones are all present — the guard that stops it from demoting genuine slash chords *(`a user-act marker`)*
- **D-538** — A multi-signal scoring change lands one signal at a time, with the corpus check re-run after each step and any increase in errors a hard stop before the next *(`a user-act marker`)*
- **D-539** — The standing method for every error slice: decompose it into structural, fitted and ceiling BEFORE building anything — derive, never assert *(`a user-act marker`)*
- **D-542** — Idiom discovery runs DISCOVER-THEN-NAME: structure is learned on a low-level encoding carrying no theory or genre labels, and theory features and genre labels are interpretation lenses applied afterwards, never clustering input *(`a user-act marker`)*
- **D-543** — The encoding is key-normalised tonal-pitch-class TRANSITIONS — spelled where spelling is reliable, mod-12 only where it is genuinely absent — run as two complementary views *(`a user-act marker`)*
- **D-544** — Confound control is a FIRST-CLASS GATE, and the source-leakage test decides validity: if the clusters are explained by which corpus a piece came from, the result is bookkeeping and not idiom *(`a user-act marker`)*
- **D-545** — The uniform mechanical extractor for idiom discovery is the external library, stopping at the note-and-slice front — OUR OWN key/chord/function inference must NEVER touch the extraction *(`a user-act marker`)*
- **D-546** — A delegation reaches only the members it names explicitly — a glob and a trailing ellipsis confer nothing; with the two confirmations ruled beside it *(`a dated user ruling named+a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-547** — An audit's scope is generated mechanically from the code and every row gets a disposition — the auditor never chooses what to look at *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-548** — The audit's verdict set is closed with no silent escape, and 'no issue' is itself a recorded claim with a stated reason *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-549** — An audit runs in BOTH directions — from the contract to the code as well as from the code to the reader's judgment — because an absence is findable only from the contract side *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-550** — Every mechanism in the audit inventory carries a MEASURED fire rate beside what it claims — reading answers what it claims, counting answers what it does *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-551** — The audit is itself established with a MEASURED residual-error rate; an audit whose error rate is unknown certifies nothing *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-552** — An audit is TWO runs in a fixed order — blind enumeration first, then the known-problem signature sweep — and certification requires both *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-557** — Build-it-right comes BEFORE tune-precision, strictly — no inference-problem fixing anywhere until the refactoring, the architectural design and the algorithmic completion are done *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-564** — Correction of record: the 'function-only' share of the legacy residual was overstated, because over-grabbed segmentation corrupts the BASS and not only the pitch-class window *(`a user-act marker`)*
- **D-565** — Exact score ties in the decode are real and are broken by a declared TOTAL ORDER on paths, implemented identically in every decoder — no epsilon, no platform dependence *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-566** — STRUCTURAL fixes come before correctness fixes: never tune an inference decision while a responsibility is still in the wrong place *(`a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-567** — An audit document labels each claim TRUTH or PROVISIONAL — a claim read from the source this session stands; any quantity carried from memory of an earlier investigation does not *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-568** — The two-track remedy: the chord axis is completed by hand-built rules, the key axis by soft-evidence quality and calibration — and NOT by a wider search on either *(`a user-act marker`)*
- **D-569** — Collecting, filtering and weighting are THREE separate responsibilities; the collection layer collects and annotates, and does nothing else *(`a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-571** — The declared-mode influence becomes a small additive hint, and SMALLNESS IS THE GATE — no separate confidence test is added *(`a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-572** — The hard post-hoc declared-mode promotion is REMOVED OUTRIGHT rather than kept in a gated form *(`a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-573** — A floor measurement that collapses is a FINDING, not a failure — it is reported, not papered over *(`a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-574** — The pass-bar for a measured change is set AFTER the baseline measurement, so the threshold is data-grounded rather than guessed *(`a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-575** — The Baroque partial-signature convention is handled by DETECTING it and reinterpreting the signature one step, not by widening the candidate family for every score *(`a user-act marker`)*
- **D-576** — The corpus root-agreement measurement UNDERSTATES the real-world quality impact of a wrong key, because root and bass are largely key-independent *(`a user-act marker`)*
- **D-580** — Two of the twelve post-scoring gates are purely-local vertical refinements and MUST survive the dissolution; the other ten dissolve into the competition *(`a numbered ruling named+a ruling record named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-581** — Information not yet consumed is NOT automatically a defect: every site is classified preserved-awaiting-consumer, lost, should-already, or unclear — and unclear is recorded for adjudication, never guessed *(`a numbered ruling named+a recorded ratifier+a ruling record named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-582** — A collapse that is RECOMPUTABLE from what is kept is not information loss — not every collapse is a defect *(`a user-act marker`)*
- **D-583** — A known deferred loss is KEPT only while it stays characterized EXACTLY, and is re-adjudicated the moment its form changes *(`a numbered ruling named+a recorded ratifier+a ruling record named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-584** — The perfect/imperfect cadence call is made on the BASS-DERIVED inversion; the soprano arrival degree is demoted to a soft optional nudge and the tool never attempts melody identification *(`a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-587** — A user-facing preset presents as a familiar genre-era label plus exemplars the user knows — never as an idiom name or an obscure exemplar; genre names are LABELS over mixtures, never axes *(`a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-588** — Preset coverage beyond the analysed corpora is three tiers with NO bare guessing — measured, editorially declared with a stated theory rationale, or self-correcting by detection *(`a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-589** — Every idiom mixture is selectable and the discovered cloud is the EVIDENCE MAP, not the boundary — each chosen point carries its evidence status *(`a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-590** — The score's own metadata is the PRIMARY home of that score's idiom mixture, and a user-set mixture is never silently overwritten by re-detection *(`a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-591** — The licence split for the style system: the ANCHORS are the shipped licence-constrained fitted parameters, and the mixture weights are free user configuration *(`a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-592** — What FINISHED means for a layer, as the user's standing bar: restructured, built, dead code resolved, legacy retired, regression- and reachable-branch-tested, specs synced — nothing left *(`a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-593** — The line inside the build-it-right firewall: plain correctness bugs are allowed now, accuracy on hard cases is not — and a KNOWN WRONG ANSWER on the key axis is on the forbidden side *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-596** — The north star: BEST means CORRECT — every obligation is judged against the ground-truth oracle and never against whether it passes a proxy gate *(`a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-598** — The style taxonomy and the per-style weights are ONE data-derived object; VALIDATION is a separate third thing that needs annotated scores and is not delivered by the clustering *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-599** — Adjudication method: apply the standing principles FIRST, and only where they do not decide is there a genuine user choice — what remains for the user is ratifying the derivation, not picking an option *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-600** — The quality-overwrite information-loss violation is TOLERATED until the gate-dissolution step and stays VISIBLE in the open-items register — tolerated is not forgotten *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-601** — Before any constant that would make two differently-scaled confidences comparable is fitted, the premise that a fitted constant CAN do so must itself pass a premise ledger and a desk simulation *(`a numbered ruling named+a recorded ratifier+a ruling record named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-602** — A layer's measurement is judged on coverage-matched accuracy AND correct abstention, never on raw coverage — abstaining on a genuinely undecidable case is a RIGHT outcome *(`a user-act marker`)*
- **D-603** — While the pipeline is being rebuilt, a behaviour-changing increment is graded DIRECTIONALLY and not against a fixed bar — meaningful comparison happens only against the fully reconstructed pipeline *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-604** — A defensible modal reading the major/minor ground truth cannot represent is a GROUND-TRUTH LIMITATION, not a defect to optimise away *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-605** — The local-key hypothesis derives from key-agnostic signals ONLY and never from the key-area grouping, which is a post-grouping of the resolved key — a hard design rule, not a preference *(`a user-act marker`)*
- **D-606** — The binding metric for the modulation detector is modulation CORRECTNESS — explicitly not the agreement percentage, which the change under test can game *(`a user-act marker`)*
- **D-611** — The tidy guardrails, in three tiers — and the dormant path and its staged scaffolding are DEFERRED-ENGAGEMENT, not dead code: removing them is a STOP *(`a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-613** — Ground truth for IMPLIED polyphony is confirmed ABSENT — do not re-search it *(`a numbered ruling named+a ruling record named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-614** — Every real difficulty-grade label source is research-only or proprietary at origin — a commercial grading feature needs a licence path or its own labels *(`a numbered ruling named+a recorded ratifier+a ruling record named+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-616** — A global tonic anchor enters key scoring at RESOLVER/SECTION scope — never as one more local term inside the window scorer, which is what re-enters the coupling that defeated the local levers *(`a user-act marker`)*
- **D-622** — The reach-back convergence PROXY was measured FALSE and dropped — the as-built tracks the leading-edge key itself and stops when it stops changing *(`a user-act marker`)*
- **D-623** — A selection-aware capability is a PARAMETER on the one orchestrator, never a sibling — the capability must not duplicate the orchestration *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-625** — Spelling presence is tested with the validity predicate, never with a non-negative test — the flat side of the line of fifths is negative and a non-negative guard silently drops it *(`a user-act marker`)*
- **D-626** — A scoring term lands together with its tuning — a dormant weight-zero term is code with no effect and no test that exercises its effect *(`a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-629** — The resolver of carried uncertain readings IS the function layer itself — there is no distinct gated box between the note layers and it *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-630** — Minimality sets the default and the burden — do not add a component; a new one is earned only by exhibiting a residual class a separable criterion resolves *(`a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*
- **D-638** — The symmetric-rotation churn is a chord-layer root ambiguity SURFACED, not caused, by a key change — the proper fix is spelling-aware root selection, never a key-layer change *(`a recorded ratifier`)*
- **D-639** — Phase 1's doc-sync half reaches a document's account of ITSELF only where that account changes how its analysis content is read — with three worked examples that are the test, and a fallback ruled with it *(`a dated user ruling named+a recorded ratifier+a user-act marker`)*

## 7a. One disagreement found while walking the population, recorded and not repaired

The data file's header declares a status vocabulary; its entries use a different spelling of one of those values. The status field is not an input to the classification, so nothing here turns on it — but a walk of the whole population found it, and a finding is surfaced rather than silently smoothed.

- **The vocabulary the data file's header declares:** `deferred`, `falsified`, `live`, `not-stated`, `shelved-with-evidence`, `superseded-by`, `superseded-in-fact`
- **The spelling its entries use that the header does not:** `not stated`
- **What was done:** NOTHING was repaired. The comparison normalizes whitespace to the hyphen the header uses, on BOTH sides, so the classification could proceed; the register's data file is byte-unchanged by this run.
- **Why it was not repaired:** This batch edits no register file, and a disagreement between a record and what it describes is evidence rather than a defect to smooth away — the rule the user ruled into `CLAUDE.md` on 2026-08-15 in place of D-231's truth half.

## 8. What this surface does NOT do, and must not be read as doing

- **It discards nothing.** Every proposal is a candidacy awaiting the user; soft-discard is
  retirement from the live record and destroys nothing (#12).
- **It edits no entry and no register file.** The data file, the rendered INDEX and the
  rendered group files are byte-unchanged by the run that produced this surface.
- **It writes no decisions-register entry**, which the filtering ruling bars until the user
  rules on this surface.
- **It grades no decision's content**, its conformance, or whether the code obeys it.
- **It performs no rulings sort, no mining, no archiving and no fact-gate admission** —
  those are the preparation phase's other outputs and none of them is started here.
